On your journey to America you prayed to your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī: “By his strong desire, the holy name of Lord Gaurāṅga will spread throughout all the countries of the Western world.”

Due to the desire of your Guru Mahārāja, you came to give us this holy name. Now by your strong desire you are working through your disciples to do the same.

Here in Scotland, we have taken up the mission of developing your book distribution and preaching on many different levels. All this is for your pleasure and glorification, offered in gratitude for your mercy and kindness upon us.

You will be pleased to know that the holy name of Gaurāṅga is being spread far and wide here in Scotland. There has been an inundation of Gaurāṅga’s name for the last few years through numerous towns and villages throughout Scotland, and it is expanding more and more through our Gaurāṅga campaign of posters, stickers, and Gaurāṅga rock *prasādam* distribution. Our Every Town and Village program is systematically covering the whole of Scotland. Gaurāṅga’s name and *harināma kīrtanas* are resounding all over. Your books are being distributed on the street, door-to-door, in businesses, and through mail-order.

You will be delighted to know that your books and articles out of your books have been advertised in various newspapers throughout Scotland, reaching out to millions of people. Your desire to see a book in every house is somehow or other being fulfilled in this way. Although we are unworthy and insignificant, by your desire you have enabled us to become of some use in your mission of delivering Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the fallen souls.

Every day at your *guru-pūjā* we sing, *ebe jaśa ghuśuk tribhuvana—*“May your fame be spread throughout the three worlds.” In our small way we have been trying to spread your fame throughout the whole of Scotland. Thus for our Centennial glorification of Your Divine Grace, 5.8 million people, almost the entire population of the country, got the opportunity to read a pamphlet that describes your life-long mission to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world. These pamphlets were either inserted into newspapers or edited as articles in the newspapers and circulated throughout Scotland, covering most of the country. Thus your name and fame have been spread throughout Scotland.

Books are the basis.  
Preaching is the essence.  
Utility is the principle.  
Purity is the force.

Prabhupāda, you are the basis, the essence, the principle, and the force by which this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is continuing to expand. Without you and your mercy, there would be no books, no preaching, no utility, and no purity. Thank you, Prabhupāda.

Your mercy is causeless. Your sweet mercy is the rain which is keeping us in line. For like children, we are dependent on this guidance, which you continuously give us through your books and your disciples. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We pray that our endeavors to distribute your books and to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness will always bring more and more pleasure to you. You have given us the highest service, the *saṅkīrtana-yajña.*

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your insignificant servants in Scotland.

## Seattle

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to your faithful servants!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once said to Nara-Nārāyaṇa Prabhu in San Francisco:

You see this little ant?…We should think that our goal should be how to make this ant Kṛṣṇa conscious.…We should go to this ant and we should lean down \[and say\] “You should chant Hare Kṛṣṇa” \[and\] give a little *prasādam*. If we do that \[share Kṛṣṇa consciousness in a kind way\] our whole movement will be a success. \[*Prabhupāda-līlā: Around the World 1967-1969 & 1972-1975,* p. 139\]

You have never left us, you are always there  
in the hearts of souls you have softened with prayer.  
We have seen your smile, we have heard your preaching,  
your glory lives forever in your teaching.

One stick will break, one man will fall.  
It is time for every one of us all  
to support his brother, to encourage another  
to march beyond the materialistic cover.

We can be kind like you if we surrender to  
your simple yet strict instructions—  
for, if we do not try to imitate  
or strive to satiate personal ambition,  
we will achieve your grace  
as a natural result of following the disciplic succession.

It took courage for you to sail the Atlantic and be our example.  
It takes courage for us to stay in this seemingly ramshackle temple.  
If we “don’t change” your ISKCON, it will never degenerate  
into some whimsical cult characterized by betrayal.

It takes book distribution in case after case,  
it takes one glance from Śrī Nīla-Mādhava’s face,  
just a morsel to taste of “Rādhā Red” chutney  
or chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa melody  
to revive our conditioned senses.  
Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg your forgiveness for our offenses.  
We pray for Kṛṣṇa’s blessings to bring  
our Vaiṣṇava community together again  
so as to purely receive and offer your mercy  
in the spirit of cooperation.

Your fallen and lowly servants in Seattle, Washington, USA.

## Secunderabad

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this auspicious day of your divine descent into this material world. All glories unto you!

What glorification can we offer? What offering can we make? What can we pray for? We insectlike Kali-yuga creatures are simply awed by your oceanic mercy. Lord Caitanya appeared along with his associates, and later on many other exalted devotees appeared, but it is you who picked us up. So can we ask anything other than causeless devotion unto your lotus feet? What other shelter is available to fallen souls like us, battered by the degraded propaganda of this age? Where else can we seek help? Who is our well-wisher other than yourself and your representatives who strictly follow in your lotus footsteps?

Therefore we simply pray that you empower us to become an effective tool in your lotus hands, so that we can dance like puppets as per your will, expressed through the instructions of your bona fide representatives. If in this small fleeting life we can engage in the service of this supremely important mission of yours, we would consider our birth a success. Also, wherever and in whichever species we may travel due to our sinful mentalities, we pray for an opportunity to be thus engaged.

The aspiring servants of your servants in Secunderabad, India.  
(written by Sahadeva dāsa)

## Sierra Leone

Dear Param Guru and Jagat Guru Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this auspicious occasion of your appearance, kindly accept our insignificant obeisances among the thousands who are equally offering their respects at your lotus feet. All glories to your Centennial activities! All glories to all the Vaiṣṇava devotees who made the Centennial of the Lord’s pure devotee a memorable occasion!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, among other things, your Centennial anniversary has proven to the world that you will live forever, that you have indeed built a house in which the whole world can live. Even here in Sierra Leone, the people were aware of your appearance when the Sierra Leone Television (SLTV /BS) showed the video of “Your Ever Well-Wisher,” a chronicle of your pastimes, to the entire population. Then there was the Mayor of Freetown welcoming the *padayātrīs* at the Presidential Lounge and the ecstatic processions of the *padayātrīs* broadcasting the holy name of Kṛṣṇa across the land. And to crown it all, your illustrious son Bhakti-tīrtha Swami, the Centennial Co-ordinator for Enhancing the Worldwide Reputation of ISKCON, visited Sierra Leone during his worldwide tours and launched his book on leadership at the Sierra Leone Parliament. So people at all levels—from the common men on the street to the contented middle class glued to their televisions to the government leaders—all are experiencing the consciousness revolution of the century spear-headed by Your Divine Grace.

Having witnessed the Centennial inauguration in India and how your Centennial activities fostered more unity in ISKCON and brought the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement more into focus as an authentic spiritual organization, we have seen how your fame has spread all over the three worlds! We now pray that we may become worthy to remain eternal instruments in the unfoldment of the vision of the previous *ācāryas* in our *paramparā* and in the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya.

We, your grandchildren in Sierra Leone, won your favor when during the Centennial year we received the honor of being the Most Improved Temple in *saṅkīrtana* activities. We pray that this Centennial blessing of yours which we received as your causeless mercy in the area of the service that pleases you the most will mark a turning point in our devotional career and propel us into greater heights of book distribution on the African continent. O Prabhupāda, kindly appear in our hearts so that we can think of nothing else except you and the service you have given to us, the *saṅkīrtana-yajña*. Prabhupāda, you are master of the boat. Prabhupāda, you are captain of the ship. All glories to Your Divine Grace. All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!!!

Your servants in Sierra Leone.

## Singapore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All Glories to Your Divine Grace!

As your humble servants trying to preach here in this land that once denied entry to your great self, we feel very much honored to be able to serve you here.

After twenty-five years of trying, we have finally received approval for the registration of your Society. This is but a small success in the long struggle to establish your greatness here. Nevertheless, by your desire we have also sent some devotees to neighboring countries to start establishing centers for the benefit of the innumerable conditioned human beings nearby.

Currently two of your devotees are trying to set up a branch in Cambodia. We pray that by your supreme guidance and mercy we will eventually be able to fulfill your desires of setting up more and more centers in this region and distributing more and more books in all the languages in this area. Our society here is getting stronger, and we have an increasing congregation and more full-time devotees. We also have more Chinese devotees participating in the mission to help preach to more and more people.

The more we understand your mood of preaching, the more we see the success. You have shown the enthusiastic preaching mood by your own example, and that example is our inspiration and our source of determination and strength for carrying out your desires. As you often stressed, “‘Impossible’ is a word in the fool’s dictionary.” We have seen the truth of this statement after all these years: simply being patient, sticking to your golden rule of chanting sixteen rounds, following all the morning programs, etc., is the key to anyone’s spiritual success, and the key to performing seemingly impossible tasks.

Often this place was written off by far too many as an impossible place; we are now beginning to see some light. There is an opportunity for Kṛṣṇa consciousness here, and we are confident that Your Divine Grace will be widely glorified here eventually.

Hoping for and anticipating that day when you will again ride with us here in the streets of Singapore, we remain  
Your lowly servants in Singapore.

## Stockholm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

With the ugly face of Kali as the background, your instructions shine like diamonds in this night of Kali-yuga. More and more it is clear how important every word is that emanates from your lotus mouth. One slip, one deviation, and the soul is plunged into the humanistic, materialistic dungeon of conditioned life. How accurate and how urgent your message is! Word by word you are pushing away the confusions in the deluded minds of the victims of this age, clearing the path for them to return back home, back to Godhead. Rejecting materialistic religiosity, economic development, sense gratification, and even liberation, you established clearly what the real goals are: Kṛṣṇa’s mercy and love for Him. These are the only goals established in your teachings, which you deliver unconditionally to even the most fallen souls.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, at the time of my initiation in Germany I couldn’t even remember the four regulative principles. I did follow them, but my mind simply failed as I was kneeling in front of your *vyāsāsana*. Compassionately you smiled, looked down at me, and said, “Oh, you forgot one?”

My heart stopped and I said, “Yeees.”

You said, “It is all right. Please chant Hare Kṛṣṇa,” and gave me my initiation beads. I still have these beads, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but your *vapu,* your personal association, is gone. But I know you are there, carefully watching the daily struggle of your disciple, tolerating, encouraging, hoping that he will give up his artificiality, his pretense. Will I ever understand the depth of your tolerance and patience?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, so much madness is around us daily. The people of today are mad. Hysterical women and arrogant men appear daily on the television screens in the windows of the town, boldly declaring their independence from God and inviting others to do the same. Children abused in their homes are the zombies of tomorrow, fighting for their stolen youth the rest of their lives. Hunting for money is the rule, and the animallike humans do not miss one opportunity to use their tongues to make froglike speeches, simply ventilating their already deluded minds.

How wonderful is this *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*! How precious are your purports! Where else is any shelter to be found? Where else is sanity to be seen? The shade of your feet is the only shelter indeed. Outside of that—simply madness.

Thank you so much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for the association of my Godbrothers and their disciples. As long I have this association, life is tolerable. Please give me more service. I want to serve you more in your ISKCON and under the shelter of my Godbrothers. There is so much to do, lifetime after lifetime.

Your servants in Stockholm, Sweden.  
(written by Maṇidhara dāsa)

## Sydney

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Prabhupāda, Savior of the Whole World!

It’s academic to argue whether or not anyone else could have done what you in fact did. You were the one who saved the whole world from gliding down to hell.

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

Consequent to your achievement, whatever others may attempt can at best simply highlight a facet of your already established glory.

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

Because you have already *done* everything, who now can be unique? Despite their chagrin, nothing but imitation is now all that remains.

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

Reflecting the sun and imitating its glory, the moon only pays homage to its own cause. In the same way, any success, if genuine, only honors you the more.

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

To glorify someone separately, outside this context of dependency, diminishes rather than exalts their credit and in the end destroys all concerned.

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

“We may dismiss all this as childish prattle, the untutored sentiment of maudlin disciples, mere nostalgia for the womb.” But beware! Our founder *is* unique. Truly the self-effulgent *ācārya*.

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your lotus feet are the boat for crossing the Kali-yuga ocean. What fool, abandoning ship, would trust in the tail of a dog to carry him across?

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

The stars are many, still the moon outshines all. If we do not see those pinprick stars, it is due not to blindness but to our absorption in the moonlight.

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

“But what of separation?” I hear. Well, grant me this premise and I’ll dare you the excuse: that separation is to love what wind is to fire, extinguishing only the small but inflaming the great!

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

Loyalty alone is the basis of love. So, for better or worse, we’ll stick to the above. And if risk there be, we’ll take our chances, not for us any deviant romances.

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

Anyway, many things it is that we may say, but one thing is sure, of which no one dare of anyone else ever say, that Śrīla Prabhupāda, and he alone, really was and really is,

Savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

From all your servants in Sydney, Australia.

## Szolnok

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on the occasion of your divine Vyāsa-pūjā celebration.

We are greeting you on this wonderful day, using the opportunity to glorify you. On book distribution we often meet people who ask us, “Who is that old man in the picture?” We can tell them you are the founder-*ācārya* of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, yet this tells too little about your personality. So we tell them you are more than a person who sometimes says something about God, that you showed the teachings in your books with your life. Out of devotion for your spiritual master you came to this sinful Western world, leaving Vṛndāvana and its spiritual calmness behind. Since you are a perfect and compassionate Vaiṣṇava, you hurt to see other people’s affliction, and so you undertook every endeavor and difficulty to teach us this transcendental message.

We wish we were able to understand this message and transmit it to all people. We are your eternal debtors. And what is the best method to repay even a fraction of this debt? We should give all people Kṛṣṇa’s teachings through your books. These are not ordinary books but your personal ecstasies, which Kṛṣṇa himself dictated to you.

Since we could never meet you in person, it is more difficult for us to really understand you. Yet you are here through your disciples, who represent you and your wish. If we properly follow and serve them, that service is nondifferent from your personal service. By the grace of your dear disciple Śivarāma Swami we have received the opportunity to be Rādhā-Śyāmasundara’s *saṅkīrtana* crew. We pray for your grace to be able to render proper and nice service to you, thus satisfying your disciple and you too.

Thank you for listening to our letter.  
Your eternal servants in Szolnok, Hungary.

## Taipei

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All Glories to Your Divine Grace! May your fame spread all over the three worlds.

While explaining a verse of the *Bhagavad-gītā* (*vyavasāyātmikā buddhir ekeha kuru-nandana, Bhagavad-gītā* 2.41), Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura points out that the order of the spiritual master is the life substance of the disciple. You have taken the order of your spiritual master in that mood. You confirm this in the purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.24.15: “The disciple should not consider whether he is going back home, back to Godhead; his first business should be to execute the order of his spiritual master. Thus a disciple should always meditate on the order of the spiritual master, and that is perfectional meditation. Not only should he meditate upon that order, but he should find out the means by which he can perfectly worship and execute it.”

We, your disciples and granddisciples in your ISKCON Taipei Center, are aspiring to execute your order. What you want is made clear by you. Some devotees received from the Centennial Ministry an award in appreciation for services rendered during the Centennial Year. It is a nicely framed document with the seven purposes of ISKCON, as you have originally stated them. We also have one such document hanging in our temple room. It helps to meditate on your desire. We pray to you for the intelligence to find the means and ways to execute your order and bring the fallen souls nearer to Kṛṣṇa.

Taiwan has a population of twenty-one million people. In 1949, when the Communist party came to power in mainland China, the Nationalists escaped and settled down in this nearby island. The Buddhist and Taoist tradition has remained much the same in this place since long ago. Here monks are respected, whereas in mainland China the people have been “reformed.”

Your disciple Vaiśeṣika dāsa observes, while distributing books in the international airports in America, that the Taiwanese tourists are special: “They are always very polite, and they generally accept the books and give good donations.”

While preaching here we go door to door to distribute your books. People often ask what kind of religion we are and, even without understanding what Kṛṣṇa consciousness is, dutifully give a donation while taking the books. Of course, people are captured by Kali and grossly absorbed in *māyā,* but still they are a bit pious. There is a spark, and we have to fan it. As you once said, “If they little appreciate, ‘Oh, these people are nice,’ that is one asset. This is called *ajñāta-sukṛti,* accumulation of the result of pious activities. And when one is fully pious, at that time he can understand what is God. So we are helping them, by and by, to advance in the matter of understanding God. This is our propaganda” (lecture on *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.16.23, Hawaii, 19 January 1974).

Our vision is to make “Hare Kṛṣṇa” a household word. Your devotees here have learned Chinese to become more expert in preaching. Sometimes we preach on the radio. Your center is in a good location near the Taiwan University, and we have *prasādam* distribution. Your BBT is reactivated, and we have a big variety of books in Chinese. We started with the cell preaching program, and gradually people are learning to practice the principles of *bhakti-yoga*. One day we hope to have a *padayātrā* to cover the whole island with the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. We take advantage of the people’s desire to travel and guide them in tours to India for visiting Kṛṣṇa’s pastime places, which you have made known to the world.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are an ocean of mercy. Thank you for engaging us in your glorious ISKCON movement.

Humbly, your servants in Taipei, Taiwan.

## Tampa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Your temples are an oasis in the dry desert of Kali-yuga. I was totally lost in the Kali-yuga city of Boston in 1972. Bewildered, unhappy, and cold, I saw your bright devotees singing and dancing at the Commons, led by Trai dāsa Prabhu.

I found your temple vibrating with pure love, and joined to be your disciple within two weeks. I felt such happiness and shelter far from the cruel, hard material existence. Your devotees taught me how to live happily, and your books taught me how to think.

Prabhupāda, your gift of love and life is so wonderful; every day is a beautiful day in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Every day I see people’s lives magically changed by the words in your books. So many people milling around the big cities, proud of all the big zeros they have accumulated, but so unhappy. You, Prabhupāda, have given us the Supreme One, Kṛṣṇa, and made all our zeros valuable. You have created the spiritual world right here in the darkest corners of Kali-yuga. By opening and reading your books we can enter Kṛṣṇa-loka, and by our distributing your books to the unsuspecting, ignorant people of Kali-yuga, the spiritual world opens for all to freely enter. This is all your mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we must blindly follow your many orders and guidance, assured of success in the perfection of life. If there is some doubt, within moments of reading your beautiful books it is cleared, as the sky is cleared of clouds by a strong wind. We aspire to be in remembrance of your words twenty-four hours a day and to keep working, always busy pushing on your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, distributing your books to every single lost suffering conditioned soul in every dark corner of this earthly planet.

We want to please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. There is nothing else to live for.

Your servants in Tampa, Florida, USA.  
(written by Vivasvān dāsa)

## Tauranga

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

As the years go by, it is becoming more and more apparent that you actually gave us everything. For any given question we may have, our only task is to find where you have spoken on it, and in relation to what circumstances. As you asked the Lord to be your choreographer, showing you how to dance and to what tune, we are asking you simply to give us the inspiration and insights by which we can always remain faithful to you. The chaste and faithful disciple, it is said, does not try to introduce new or conflicting statements but rather finds in the vastness of *pramāṇa* those truths that support the teachings of Your Divine Grace.

What do we know without you, where would we be without you? We dread to think, but we think of that nonetheless to remind us of our gratitude to you.

Today it is popular to make affirmations, think positively, to see with creative vision, etc., yet I suggest that these are not new things. You created a society in which the *sādhana* of the morning and evening programs is made of positive—truly positive—affirmations declaring who we are and what we need to aspire for. They protect us against the negative influences of this temporal world. Your creative vision, through implicit faith in *guru,* revealed to you many instances of how the Lord had already fulfilled His plan, just as He revealed it to Arjuna—you only had to act, victory was assured. You executed the Lord’s plan, and you are entitled to take the full glory, because it was sculptured by your pure desire, your creative vision: “There are so many temples, so many devotees, so many books,” you said, “just there is time separating us.” What could be more revealing of your total dedication, what could be more rewarding when the time was right?

There is nothing hidden that needs to be found. You have made everything accessible to all, especially to those who have the qualifications. The qualifications are to see through your eyes, to be Prabhupādānugas, using all we find, held up to the template of your infallible teachings, which are set to guide us through the next ten thousand years. The onus is now on us to use your medical journals to maintain and keep our health, by which we can again resume eternal life. Ill health means not knowing what to do, or how to be healthy, falling prey to strange ideas that come and go, testing and tempering the desire of us *sādhakas* to be healthily situated.

Please always find ways to give us the medicine. Desperately we need to be cured, to wash off the external diseases of bodily misidentification. The dreamers need to awake. Please strengthen our fledgling community here, bestow your blessings upon all of us, that we may be of some use to you, and be of some use to ourselves in the process.

Please accept this humble offering of sincere gratitude,  
From your disciples and granddisciples in Tauranga, New Zealand.  
(written by Jaya Tīrtha Caraṇa dāsa).

## Tokyo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

We think you don’t know us, or maybe you do.  
But when you went by we got a glimpse of you.

We first saw you in the garden in Glebe.  
You planted in our hearts the devotional seed.

As you looked at your Lord, we stood beside you,  
And the joy in our hearts just grew and grew.

The love for Kṛṣṇa that emanates from you  
Makes us eager to taste that love too.

As we walked near you on a morning walk,  
All our doubts went away as we heard you talk.

*Bhagavad-gītā* with purports, too—  
Purple abridged—we read it all through.

To distribute your books, we went out one day.  
It was ecstatic we found, not like work, but like play.

Please give us your glance; that’s all that we pray.  
And maybe soon we can meet again one day.

Your servants in Tokyo, Japan.

## Trinidad

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet on this glorious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who appeared in this mortal world to fulfill the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu that “In every town and village the chanting of My name will be heard.”

In your poem on-board the *Jaladuta,* you stated, “By Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura’s strong desire, the holy name of Lord Gaurāṅga will spread throughout all the countries of the Western world.” By your meditation on his strong desire, you fulfilled that desire, despite old age, ill health, and many other difficulties.

You have done what no one else could have done: you made thousands of Vaiṣṇavas all over the world. And although you have physically left us, you are still spiritually present in your ISKCON, continually inspiring all devotees to preach on your behalf. You stressed, “Stick to the regulative principles, preach sincerely, and success is guaranteed.”

Your dedicated disciples have made sure that the present generation of granddisciples and all other generations to come for the next ten thousand years can know you. They’ve done this by producing your *Līlāmṛta*, your films—“Your ever well-wisher” and “The Final Lesson”—recorded lectures, your books, all other books glorifying you, your recorded *bhajans,* your *samādhis,* and your *mūrti*. This guarantees that your granddisciples and future disciples will have the opportunity to develop a loving personal relationship with you.

By your mercy, your Centennial celebrations, shown on television here, were the biggest ever in the history of Trinidad ISKCON, where many government ministers glorified you. So to preserve this great legacy you have left us, in remembrance of you and in a spirit of cooperation, we are continuing our preaching programs, *harināmas*, and the study of your books, which will forever remain the basis of all our endeavors.

We humbly bow down at your lotus feet and beg to remain your menial servants birth after birth.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your insignificant servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādha-Gopīnātha Mandira in Longdenville, Trinidad.*

## Tucson

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

To make proper advancement in this age,  
We must serve an advanced great sage.  
Your happiness is our only wage,  
Life after life in your service we’ll engage.

Your instructions ring true and clear;  
Only a fool would have any fear.  
Your life for us is beyond compare.  
Someone greater than you? I’m not aware.

Prabhupāda, please keep me on track.  
If I swerve, please pull me back.  
With my restless mind, I may become slack;  
After so many years I still don’t have the knack.

Don’t allow my desire to serve you wane;  
Keep my goal fixed on the topmost plane.  
From your instructions I have all to gain—  
Your divine message for the simple and the sane.

Preaching is purifying, if we keep that intent,  
Giving ourselves fully, one hundred percent.  
Letting go of *anarthas,* nothing to lament,  
Falling out of your grace—I have to prevent.

I’ve got to get serious, time’s running out;  
Life is shifting around us, there is no doubt.  
Chaos and uncertainty, *māyā’s* all about:  
Following all your instructions is my only safe route.

No need to be confused;  
Simple living must not be refused.  
Reduce, rethink, and reuse—  
Devotees alert, no time to amuse.

When the end of the millennium shows its ill effect,  
And Mother Bhūmi can’t tolerate the gross neglect,  
And global *karma’s* waiting to collect,  
May I take full shelter of my master, most perfect.

Your fallen servants in Tucson, Arizona, USA.  
(written by Saṇḍāmiṇī-devī dāsī)

## Turkey

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Please accept our most humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are empowered by Lord Kṛṣṇa to speak the message of God and deliver His love to those who can hear you. To those who can hear you, you never stop speaking. Nowadays you speak even more loudly through your tapes, books, disciples, and granddisciples. As you shower the mercy of Kṛṣṇa’s love upon this burning earth, we stand in awe before your grandeur, glory, and endless soothing grace.

Let us reflect upon our grand–spiritual master, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktivedanta Swami Mahārāja Prabhupāda, who is our very life and reason for existence. We pray that we can always remember him in our attempt to go back to Godhead. Wherever he travels he goes in the one absolute direction, and we beg that he takes us with him. Please let us cling to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you are the perfection of the soul. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are wandering lost and bewildered, thrown hither and thither by the cruel currents of material life. Please give us shelter at your lotus feet. You carry within your heart the effulgent personality of Godhead. And your presence is a veritable holy place where all may be purified. You have shown the world that your life belongs only to Kṛṣṇa, and your words are never different from your actions. In this universe there is nothing so pure and sublime as your mission. As such you can attract the lowest mind to have faith in Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. O standard of the universe! Please purify our wretched beings so that we can give some service to the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya.

Because you are Kṛṣṇa’s eternal associate, dearest friend, and pure devotee, you are situated in complete and perfect truth. In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* it is described that the nature of the Absolute Truth is His ability to release people from the miseries of material existence. Even a blind man can see how you have brought fortune and auspiciousness to the most fallen souls. O universal teacher and perfect guide, please let us share in your wisdom and help you relieve the burden of the world.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are like the rain cloud that pours the mercy of love of God even on the rocks. Each time it rains the same rocks again receive water. You are so powerful that even rocks melt and feel love of God. You are unlimited in mercy. Even though offenses are constantly committed at your lotus feet, you still offer blessing after blessing. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are just like Lord Nityānanda. May our stone hearts melt in the glow of your compassion.

Because the ocean is so deep, the disturbances on the surface cannot reach into its depth. In the same way, there is no way to measure how deep you have gone into the loving affairs of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, or how much you are feeling for your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. He emphasized that in time you would do everything alone. You are the topmost *rasika* Vaiṣṇava, and in your books, lectures, and conversations you have given us everything necessary for achieving our eternal *svarūpa* and *rasa*. We do not need anyone else to lead us to this supreme perfection. Those who are sincere can easily understand this, because you clearly said, “Everything is in my books.” Let our minds never deviate from the straight path you mercifully gave us; let us stick fully to your divine instructions and thus become deep in love for Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you are aware of the supreme plan of the Absolute Lord, you single-handedly fulfilled all the predictions of the previous *ācāryas*. You fulfilled the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu by manifesting this spiritual movement, which is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Nowadays Kṛṣṇa has manifest as ISKCON. And you gave us simple instructions: “Remain in ISKCON \[which means remain in Kṛṣṇa\] and Lord Caitanya will personally take you back to Godhead.” We do not need any other instructions. You are ISKCON’s *saṁsthāpaka* and *rasika ācārya*. Your words and your promises are as good as Kṛṣṇa’s words and promises. Kṛṣṇa said, *mā śucaḥ:* “Do not worry.” Why should we worry? Just by following your clear and simple instructions we will definitely achieve the highest perfection of life, *kṛṣṇa-prema*. Now, please, let us take shelter in your instructions and your simplicity. Let us drink the nectarean waters of your forgiveness and learn how to love.

You have said that a devotee would much rather dance with Kṛṣṇa than merge with Him. Thus you are inviting us to the spiritual sky, where all the perfect beings are eternally engaged in their dance of love, free of any tinge of material inebriety. Though during your latter days you appeared like an old man, you are actually an eternal youth always dancing with Kṛṣṇa. Both the most erudite and the most unintelligent can easily take refuge in your humble request: “Come back home to play with your dearest friend, whom you have forgotten.” That friend, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Lord of everyone’s heart, is known most intimately by you. We were all dried up in the burning desert of mental speculation and sense gratification. You have presented us with an unlimited ocean of immortal nectar and made us eligible to enter into Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa’s eternal pastimes. And now, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who are the perfect example of humility, patience, tolerance, compassion, and undeviating, cent-percent submission at the lotus feet of your Guru Mahārāja, are leading us up out of the realm of the living dead. You are offering to us that gem after which all conditioned souls are searching for millions upon millions of births, the highest gift: eternally perfect, absolutely unconditional, and unlimitedly ecstatic hankering for the intimate association of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava.

You have called ISKCON a sample of Vaikuṇṭha, the spiritual abode, where there is no anxiety, where everyone is constantly remembering Kṛṣṇa—the cowherd boys, the *gopīs,* the beasts, the trees, and the insects. Actually you distributed Vṛndāvana everywhere, but at the same time you kindly left for us some places where we can engage in the mission of Lord Caitanya. Now with all our endeavors we have concentrated on Istanbul because His Holiness Vipramukhya Swami informed us that you personally instructed him to open a temple in Istanbul. Words of a pure devotee never fail. Therefore the wonderful temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Istanbulīśvara is already there, and only time separates us from it. You are just waiting for us to become qualified enough, and then you will reveal this temple. Kṛṣṇa told Arjuna that all warriors on the Battlefield of Kurukṣetra had already been killed by Him, but He wanted Arjuna to fight and get the credit; similarly, you have already done everything, but you have kindly left us some work so that we can be engaged in your mission and be purified.

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, in His pastimes with the Pāṭhāna Muslims and Chand Kazi, had already shown us how to preach to Muslims. Later on, through your books, lectures, conversations, and activities, you gave us vivid examples of how to execute Mahāprabhu’s mission. But we are so weak, dull, and useless that we cannot follow your clear instructions without deviation. Therefore we take guidance of your bona fide pure representative, His Holiness Harikeśa Mahārāja, to preach successfully in Muslim countries like Turkey, Iraq, Jordan, Syria and Egypt. Under his expert guidance we succeed. Now many people in these countries read your books and chant the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra*. We also promote congregational preaching under the guidance of another of your pure representatives, His Holiness Jayapatāka Mahārāja. By following his elaborate instructions, we have been quite successful in congregational preaching, which is the main preaching technique in Muslim countries. Those who take shelter of these two main pillars of ISKCON will never fail. May you be pleased by our offerings and activities under guidance of your bona fide representatives. May you bless us through them.

We are also pushing forward a global preaching project called “Sanātana-dharma for the Muslim World.” Part of this project is aimed at bringing various Muslim scholars and leaders from such Muslim countries as Turkey, Syria, Iraq, Jordan, Iran, and Egypt to Śrīdhām Māyāpur to discuss spiritual topics, to share interfaith realizations, and to give them a clearer perspective on Kṛṣṇa consciousness and the goals of the Māyāpur project. We are trying to fulfill your desire that people of all nations and creeds can come to Śrīdhām Māyāpur and learn various subjects from ISKCON devotees.

We are praying, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we shall always possess the strength and determination to never deviate from your sacred service and the straight path you gave us. In this way, and only in this way, will we ever be able to fulfill our duty, which is to fully accept and pass on to others the gift you are offering. Please accept our heads, bodies, minds, words, wealth, hearts, and souls at your divine lotus feet, and do with them as you wish; we aspire to become perfect tools in your hands.

O Śrī Prahlāda-Narasiṁha, O Śrī Pañca-tattva, O Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava and the *aṣṭa-sakhīs,* please hear our humble prayers. Please make us fit to fulfill all of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s desires and plans. Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories, all glories, all glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your most insignificant servants in Turkey.  
(written by Airāvata dāsa)

## Udhampur

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Hare Kṛṣṇa. Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus like feet. All glories to your divine personality, which attracts the most fallen souls to your lotus feet and saves them from the blazing fire of this material existence.

We were all suffering in this material world, but by your causeless mercy we are now engaged in devotional service continuously. Your mercy is limitless, and our conditioning is also limitless. Through your true representative, His Holiness Nava Yogendra Swami Mahārāja, you have given us a new project—the *gurukula* at Jammu—for which we find ourselves most unqualified. But we are confident and convinced of your potency; you are a *śaktyāveśa avatāra,* and if you desire you can empower anyone to carry out your transcendental mission.

We pray to Your Divine Grace on this most blessed day, the anniversary of your appearance, to empower these menial servants of yours to accomplish this task as soon as possible.

We are so insignificant that, even though seeing your miracles, we fail to glorify a personality like yourself.

We always feel your presence in this *āśrama,* and whoever visits your *āśrama* here at Udhampur returns home with a special realization of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We are so unqualified that in the last ten years we couldn’t build a temple for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, and Your Divine Grace, but we still have some hope that you are pleased by our humble service. We have no words to thank you. We are indebted to you birth after birth for all that you have given us.

Once again we beg you to help us become a little qualified to understand Your Divine Grace and your divine mission.

Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

The servants of your servant in Udhampur, India.

## Umea

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to your appearance in this material world!

By the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, Your Divine Grace came to the Western countries. You were alone and had no assistance, but you always felt that you were in the association of your spiritual master. It was your desire that your disciples would follow in your footsteps by opening centers and preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness for the benefit of the fallen conditioned souls in all parts of the world. In pursuance of that order and with the desire to attain your mercy by pleasing Your Divine Grace, we are pushing on a very humble preaching center here in the north of Sweden.

Recently we were doing a school program in a small town that is about a twelve-hour train ride north of Stockholm, that is to say, nearly on the Arctic Circle. For your pleasure I would like to describe a passage written by an elementary school student at that remote place. This passage, contained in a paper about Kṛṣṇa consciousness he wrote for his comparative religion class, describes Your Divine Grace. First he quoted very nicely from your poem *Markine Bhāgavata-dharma*. Then, summarizing his description of Your Divine Grace, he wrote:

(translated from Swedish) “In the poem he prayed for help, and that he got also, I can practically guarantee. Because everything that he did at that old age would not be possible for an ordinary person without help from higher powers. Nonetheless, Prabhupāda was a humble man, and he considered that everything he accomplished was not so extraordinary. But one thing that he was proud about was *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*. And who can blame him for that?”

This direct and simple appreciation of Your Divine Grace, with clear understanding of your mission, is possible only by the combined mercy of *guru* and Kṛṣṇa. This keeps me alive, and assures me once again that our tiny attempt to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness here is very important. Hoping for your blessings to carry on in the coming year,

Your servants in Umea, Sweden.  
(written by Yogīndra dāsa)

## Uppsala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**The Donor**

Listen, seeker of truth,  
Lament no more:  
Your ship in search of love  
Soon will reach the shore.

I know you’ve been drowning  
In an ocean of pain;  
Come heal your wounded heart  
In this soothing rain.

Just like a shadow, follow  
A form of splendid youth;  
Find hidden treasures,  
Far beyond common truth.

See the King of Dancers  
Ecstatically moving along,  
Captivating each mind  
With the most wonderful song.

Sweet sidelong glances  
Shoot from His restless eyes;  
Fountains of tears gushing forth,  
In deep love He cries,

More brilliant than the moon,  
Lovelier than a lotus flower,  
Stealing the hearts of all  
With His astonishing power.

Millions of Cupids  
Fall flat at His feet.  
Totally enchanted by love,  
They acknowledge defeat.

This supreme secret was given  
By the most merciful hand.  
Please don’t reject it.  
Be eager to understand

The foremost giver of gifts,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

Your menial servants in Uppsala, Sweden.  
(written by Prapūjaka dāsa)

## Vienna

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Please accept our most humble obeisances into the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to ISKCON, which was founded by you for the deliverance of the whole world.

Kṛṣṇa’s plan is completely transcendental, free of any material contamination. His plan is free of envy, greed, lust, and illusion. He wants to deliver the fallen souls, but not because He needs them. Kṛṣṇa is *svarāṭ,* completely independent. Kṛṣṇa does not at all need our service. So why does He send His intimate associate Śrīla Prabhupāda to canvass, “Go back to Kṛṣṇa!”? The answer can only be: Because he loves us. He needs our service because this service will make us happy. Kṛṣṇa is not demanding anything for Himself. He does not need anything. He just wants our love because by loving Him we will partake in His transcendental nature of *sac-cid-ānanda,* and thus we will become eternally happy and blissful in our relationship with the Lord.

A pure devotee serves Kṛṣṇa out of love. Being one with Kṛṣṇa’s transcendental desire, he completely depends on Kṛṣṇa and on Kṛṣṇa’s representative, the spiritual master. The Lord could accomplish everything without the help of anyone else, but He feels obliged by His devotee’s eagerness to serve Him. For example, in the Battle of Kurukṣetra the Lord could have easily destroyed all the warriors on the enemy side without any help. But Kṛṣṇa wanted His devotee Arjuna to perform the task because He wanted to see him glorified. Kṛṣṇa feels obliged by His devotee’s unalloyed love and his eagerness to render pure, unmotivated service.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are such an unalloyed lover of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Your glory is that you love Kṛṣṇa and His devotees. Kṛṣṇa arranged everything so that in the end your effort was victorious. So you succeeded in everything because of your infinite love for Kṛṣṇa.

Now, one could say, “Everything was easy to accomplish for such a personality as Śrīla Prabhupāda. Kṛṣṇa helped him in every respect, and Kṛṣṇa empowered him with his energies. All the energies and opulences necessary stood at his disposal. But I am an ordinary person and I cannot do much.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you never declared that you are independent from Kṛṣṇa. You never said that you have independent power. But you acted as the perfect tool of Kṛṣṇa. You are completely surrendered unto Him, and thus being completely and totally free of envy, you can perceive and taste the all-merciful plan of Kṛṣṇa, which is on the same spiritual platform, devoid of all material impurities of exploitation. Your greatness is not that you say that it is your power but that you always use Kṛṣṇa’s power in His service, without expecting even a tinge of gain for yourself. God is God not only because He is great and powerful. He is even more God because He is the supremely loving entity. In the same way, your most important quality is not that your are powerful. Your most important quality is that you ever use your independence in the most wonderful way to love and serve your eternal Lord, Kṛṣṇa, and by this love you spiritually force Kṛṣṇa to supply you with all necessary energies for His service. And these energies stay at your service. Thus you are a supremely empowered devotee of the Lord.

You want us to become empowered, too. You want us to love Kṛṣṇa, too. To say “I am not empowered, I cannot do much for you and Kṛṣṇa” is simply an excuse and a way to cheat oneself. If we are to avoid this cheating we have to surrender and give up our false pride and ownership. We have to become completely dependent on you, just as you are dependent on your spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa. We have to make our desire one with Kṛṣṇa’s desire and your desire. We have to become humble. You are a transparent medium, never standing in the way of Kṛṣṇa’s plan. That is your unlimited glory, and therefore you are forever intimately dear to Kṛṣṇa. We are not able to grasp even one spark of the love you feel for Kṛṣṇa and that Kṛṣṇa feels for you.

We can simply throw ourselves into the dust of your lotus feet and pray for your mercy. Here in Austria we are heavily missing one thing: humility. We don’t want to be humble and meek, simply depending on your mercy. We want to control and lord it over. We think that we are very strong. Therefore we are always falling flat on the nose. We are unable to cooperate, being the enjoyers and controllers. We do not follow in your footsteps; we do not follow your instructions. We have manufactured our own way and standard.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are more merciful than the Lord Himself. We are honestly and sincerely praying: By your mercy, we know that we have a big problem. Therefore we implore you with all the little faith we have: Somehow or other let us learn to give up our wrong identification with our body and mind, our wrong concept of being the enjoyer and controller, and let us learn as fast as possible to simply follow in your footsteps, follow your divine instructions, desiring nothing else than to become your perfect tool for spreading this mission in this country, just as you have shown us by your example. Thus we will become happy, because in this way you will be happy with us.

Your fallen servants of Vienna /Gutenstein, Austria.  
(written by Jagannidhi dāsa)

## Villaggio Hare Krishna

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet!

We can see that the greatest problem for conditioned souls is the cycle of repeated birth, disease, old age, and death.

However, we realize that only your grace and guidance will help us overcome these fourfold miseries and everything related to them. Death, always chasing us along with suffering and fear, is overcome by your reassuring and sweet words. In your presence we can win over meanness and wickedness.

By your teachings you strengthen our faith and support our hopes. Your exhortations to not worry about the things of this world are like food for those who sincerely follow you.

The message that you brought leads us to hope, to the revival of new transcendental horizons and new meanings to reach, giving up wrongs and pseudo certitudes, accepting risks and struggles. Your message is the key that opens the door to enlightenment for all those who are searching for spiritual perfection. By accepting your instructions, one can really achieve perfect knowledge and give actual meaning to one’s life. The world nowadays feels more and more the need for peace.

Wars and social and racial conflicts fill everyone’s heart with anxiety. You came and, with your movement, offered the way leading to real peace and prosperity for the world. Our desire is to fulfill more and more your wish: to give people the chance to know Kṛṣṇa.

Again and again we humbly pray to you that, in fulfilling this mission, our conviction will not dwindle and our sincerity will increase.

Your humble servants at Villaggio Hare Krishna, Italy.  
(written by Valihara dāsa)

## Villa Vṛndāvana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your pure lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace again and again!!

Again here are your immature sons and daughters offering you feelings of gratitude. Thank you very much for your tireless preaching all over the world. One day this entire planet will understand who you really are and will feel infinitely indebted to Your Divine Grace.

On this auspicious occasion of the celebration of your appearance on this planet, our minds are especially concentrated on perhaps your most important instruction to us: “Please do your duty combinedly without any disruption of peaceful attitudes amongst yourselves. We are pushing our movement on the background of a peaceful atmosphere in the world, and if we show a little disturbance in our own camp that will not be a very good example. Therefore, everyone should be forbearing, tolerant, and cooperative. *That is my request to you all*” (Letter, 18 January 1969; emphasis added).

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, of all your instructions, maybe this is the most difficult one to follow. We are chanting sixteen rounds of *japa* and following the four regulative principles every single day, and it is becoming more and more easy to do it. But to follow the instruction to work in a cooperative spirit among ourselves is not always so easy.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, every day we are offering the following prayers to the Lord:

“Please, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, enable us to obey the crest jewel of all teachings of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu—*tṛṇād api sunīcena*. In this way enable us to fulfill this instruction of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s: ‘Please do your duty combinedly without any disruption of peaceful attitudes amongst yourselves.’

“Please, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, enable us to offer unto the lotus feet of our grand–spiritual master, on this very auspicious occasion, the *gift of obedience to his instructions*.

“Please, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, help us surrender more and more to the preaching mission of His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda, the real savior of the world!”

These are very big prayers for such small persons as ourselves, but *this is our duty* and we must do it! There is no alternative!!

The aspiring servants of your servants in Villa Vṛndāvana, Italy.  
(written by Dayānidhi dāsa)

## Zagreb

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

One year has passed since the magnificent celebration of the Centennial of your appearance. The melodious rhythms of deep attachment focusing on you are still resounding in our hearts. At the very beginning of the celebration, with your aristocratic arrival on the palanquin, you aroused strong emotions in all those present. When you were carefully seated on the *vyāsāsana* in the middle of the grandiose lotus flower, you were actually seated in our hearts.

That evening you clearly showed us that everything making our lives spiritual and exalted—the *mahā-mantra,* your books, co-operation with other devotees, serving the Vaiṣṇavas, preaching—all of this is simply your causeless mercy upon all of us, who had recently been drowning in the ocean of material existence. The uninformed might ask, “Why is Śrīla Prabhupāda so magnetically attractive?” The answer is simple: Śrīla Prabhupāda is *tadīya,* one who belongs to Kṛṣṇa. The Lord’s most confidential servant, the spiritual master, as well as everything else connected with the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, is *tadīya* and worthy of everyone’s worship. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who are bound to Śrī Kṛṣṇa with unbreakable ropes of pure love and who are binding all of us with the same ropes—aren’t you the one who is most worthy of our worship? Through your books you are instructing us how to overcome different obstacles on the spiritual path, such as *kuṭi-nāṭi* (faultfinding and duplicity), *pratiṣṭhāśā* (hankering for name, fame and high position), *niṣiddhācāra* (accepting things forbidden in *śāstras*), *kāma* (hankering for material profit), *pūjā* (hankering for popularity) and *jīva-hiṁsā* (envy of other living beings). You explained *jīva-hiṁsā* in the following way: “The word *jīva-hiṁsā* (envy of other living entities) actually means stopping the preaching of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Preaching work is described as *paropakāra,* welfare activity for others. Those who are ignorant of the benefits of devotional service must be educated by preaching. If one stops preaching and simply sits down in a solitary place, he is engaging in material activity” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā* 12.135, purport).

How fortunate we are to be engaged in the mission of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness under the guidance of your swanlike disciples, our *gurus*! With their purity and devotion they are the proof of your spiritual strength and unfathomable transcendental importance for all of us. Thank you for the preaching engagements by which you are teaching us still greater humility, better communication, and more correct relations among Vaiṣṇavas. Thank you for giving us the shelter of ISKCON, the place of our transcendental maturation. O spiritual grandfather, you know that the grandfather is more merciful than the father himself. Therefore all of us grandsons and granddaughters of yours beg you today, on this all-auspicious day, to grant us ample enthusiasm and strength in our spiritual life and preaching activities. Let your blessings make the whole of Zagreb, the whole of Croatia, and the whole world happy in Kṛṣṇa consciousness! Let all unanimously and joyfully sing “Jaya Prabhupāda! Jaya Prabhupāda!”

Your granddisciples and aspiring granddisciples in Zagreb, Croatia.

## Zurich

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda

Please accept our most humble obeisances. Again and again we fall at your lotus feet because you are most dear to Kṛṣṇa. By your love of Kṛṣṇa you have obtained Kṛṣṇa, and thus you are able to give Kṛṣṇa to everyone. While offering our obeisances, we know that we are not alone. Wonderful devotees from all over the world, and from all three worlds, are glorifying Your Divine Grace for having revealed Śrī-Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, at this most crucial point of time—in the middle of the day of Brahmā, in the middle of Brahma’s life. At this turning point, you have appeared.

The more the Kali-yuga advances, the more we realize the greatness of your personality and the greatness of the service you have rendered for the eternal benefit of all the fallen souls within this universe. Demons are trying to impose their totalitarian power upon this planet, and materially speaking, we are defenseless. They have usurped all the powerful positions. But you never got hung up with them, although you must have been the greatest threat to their plans. With full faith in Lord Kṛṣṇa, His holy name, and Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva, you boldly preached the absolute truth and the *yuga-dharma,* as revealed by Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda. By your chanting and preaching, the process of spiritualizing this planet has been started, which will raise the mass consciousness more and more until the age of Lord Caitanya will be fully manifested and the demoniac influence cleansed away from the planet.

Your vision was perfect, and your plans were ingenious. All your prophecies will come true, as you are in direct contact with Kṛṣṇa. There are so many examples testifying to this fact. Let us cite one example, which, though small, is very meaningful for us devotees here in Zurich.

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā* 16.281, you wrote, “Presently, the members of the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement throughout the world live in materially opulent cities, such as London, New York, Los Angeles, Paris, Moscow, Zurich, and Stockholm. However, we are satisfied with following in the footsteps of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura and other *ācāryas*. Because we live in the temples of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa and continuously hold *hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana*—the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa—we consequently live in Vṛndāvana and nowhere else.”

You dictated these lines sometime in the first half of 1974, before you were scheduled to visit our Switzerland temple in Geneva at the end of May that year. There was a nice temple in Geneva, with many devotees, and you were about to visit that place. Still, in your purport you did not mention Geneva but *Zurich,* although at this time we had only a small apartment there, a temporary preaching base that was soon given up.

But your vision was perfect. In 1981 we actually moved to Zurich, where we had purchased an aristocratic three-story building that became the base of the Swiss *yatra*. There was no temple in Geneva anymore. In the eighties the temple in Zurich, which you foresaw and chose to mention in your purport, became the world’s leader in book distribution, maintaining the fire you had ignited, till it spread out again all over the world, “just like wildfire.”

We devotees at the Zurich temple are very thankful we are allowed to live in the association of your empowered disciples and granddisciples, who are manifesting your vision more and more. It is by your causeless mercy alone that we can witness the manifestation of Śrī-Śrī Gaura-Nitāi’s *līlā* with our own eyes. It is wonderful, and it is the direct proof of your being the empowered representative of Lord Caitanya on this planet, sent to inundate this universe with ecstatic love of God.

We are unqualified and conditioned, but somehow or other, by your mercy, we have become *bhāgyavān-jīva*. Therefore, we beg to find the shelter of your lotus feet, which will protect us from false ego, complacency, and friction. In this way we will be able to attract more and more souls to your service and manifest the wonderful atmosphere radiating from your books, which are nondifferent from you.

By sticking to your books (studying and distributing them), we are sure to have your association eternally.

Your blissful servants in Zurich, Switzerland.

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from the Publishers

## The Bhaktivedanta Book Trust (Mumbai)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Your books are being sold in ever-increasing quantities in India. You will be happy to know that full sets are also being sold.

All of your books are now available in Hindi. And soon, by your grace, all the languages of India will have all of your books in print. By your desire it is happening. That is the power of your pure devotion.

Our hope is that we may be of some assistance to you in publishing your books, which are meant to create a revolution in the lives of the misdirected people of this fallen age. In your books are the keys to open the doors of devotion to Kṛṣṇa. Our hope is that we may one day do something to qualify ourselves to obtain your mercy. Without your mercy, where would we be?

We, your followers here at the Bombay BBT, hope to be able to serve you life after life by publishing your books. We have firm faith that your books are the top of the line.

Please continue to engage us in your service.

Your servants at the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust offices in Mumbai, India.

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust (North America)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

It’s six-thirty in the morning. The birds sing sweetly, and peacocks sound their exotic meow. The air resounds with *japa,* chanted by your faithful disciples. Two devotees softly sing the *Brahma-saṁhitā* on the outer verandah, upstairs in your Vṛndāvana quarters, waiting. You ring the bell and one devotee enters the inner verandah, offers obeisances, and enters the mosquito net on the bed to massage your heart. You speak softly about ISKCON matters—Bombay, Māyāpur, New Vṛndāvana, etc. Management is *arcanam,* you once said. Your servant helps you to your bath and personally bathes your transcendental form. Then he brings you to the indoor room. You sit in a rocking chair as he helps you with your *kurtā,* and you apply *tilaka* with your Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma compact. The other servant changes the linens and brings the bed onto the outer verandah, along with pillows, scented garland, bouquet, handkerchief, *lota,* incense, and *cāmara*. He had also brought fresh cloth and *kaupīna* for you. You are then aided to the outer verandah, where you sit on the bed, garlanded by your loving disciples. The incense is sweet as one devotee fans you with a *cāmara* in a figure-eight fashion, not for cooling but for keeping the numerous flies from your thin form. A few *sannyāsīs* come, and you speak to them shortly. One is sent to Nepal and told a story of having the courage of an Englishman and the heart of a Bengali mother. A letter is read from Srilanka. Your disciples have challenged the scientists: “You create one fly, one mouse, or one cat, and we’ll give you so many lakhs of rupees; otherwise you must pay.” You approve heartily. Another letter is read, from two of your men from Sweden traveling in Yugoslavia, following your program of festival, feast, and book distribution. Fifteen hundred books sold, and the people all left with stomachs full, hands full, and purses empty. You laugh strongly and reply, “ This news is making me forget my disease.”

All the devotees go except the fly-chaser, and you close your eyes for a very long time, silently taking in the August Vṛndāvana breeze and reflecting on the news. The attendant constantly fans, and from time to time you ask for the top electric fan to be turned on, or for the spittoon, or for a specific disciple. At various times the *kavirāja* is announced, and before he comes in you motion for your socks and your *chaddar* to cover your lap. He had requested you to wear them constantly, and you act like you are doing just that. By nine-thirty, devotees come up the stairs with a *tulasī* garland and help you into your rocking-chair palanquin. As they carry you down the stairs for *darśana,* the *gurukula* boys sing *kīrtana* all around you. As you enter the temple you chant “Nitāi-Gaura! Rādhe-Śyāma! Jaya Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma!” After greeting the Deities, you are brought under the *tamāla* tree as sixty or so devotees chant at your feet and the *gurukula* boys dance before you. You motion to your assistants to give the boys the *tulasī* garland and other fragrant garlands as you mercifully glance upon the entire scene.

Soon you are carried back to your ground-floor room by your *dayitās,* and you spend the day meeting with guests, *kavirājas,* and disciples. Massage is also given during the day. At about seven-thirty you are again carried upstairs to your bed on the inner verandah, where an elder disciple reads from *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* or *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* with a small lamp. You talk of going to London and America to give the Western devotees a chance to be strengthened.

Please strengthen me, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Take me with you, please. Allow me to do something nice for you and enter into your transcendental pastimes.

Your devotees at the offices of the North American Bhaktivedanta Book Trust.

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust (Northern Europe)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances unto your holy lotus feet. All glories unto you on the auspicious day of your appearance.

You remain ever present in your instructions and in your ISKCON movement. By your *vāṇī* association, you are always available to all sincere souls. Devotees all over the world will glorify you for years to come.

You have given us a clear and direct process to attain the perfection of life, personally guaranteeing that if we follow this sublime path we will go back to Kṛṣṇa, back where we belong. Who can really appreciate the gift of your causeless mercy? You have made pure, spiritual consciousness a tangible reality for us fallen Westerners, giving us the highest mission of human life: *premā pum-artho mahān*, attaining love of Godhead.

You have given us the holy name and the many-faceted wealth of Vaiṣṇava culture. You have shown us how real renunciation means using everything we can in Kṛṣṇa’s service in unlimited ways. Yet you taught us that books are the basis and instructed us to worship these books by reading them, producing them, and distributing them in great quantities throughout the world.

The knowledge that has emanated from your lotus mouth—now in the form of your precious books, the receptacles of your emotional ecstasies—is absolute and supreme. Only you could present this knowledge to us, choosing every word with the utmost care. Your books are the only hope for us to understand Kṛṣṇa and devotional service.

We are so thankful that you accepted the order of your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, to preach in the West as your life and soul. Only you could deliver us lost and ignorant souls and, touching our hearts, make us Vaiṣṇavas. You are our savior. Therefore we worship you daily as the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON and the BBT. The best worship, however, is to humbly follow your instructions.

By Kṛṣṇa’s extraordinary mercy, we are engaged in the intimate service of producing your books. Daily we are immersed in the wonderful river of nectar that flows from your books, a river more nectarean than the heavenly Jambū-nadī river of rose apple juice. Taking it directly from the source and drinking it, we become intoxicated by the unlimited sweetness of your transcendental ecstasies. Nothing is more valuable than the wealth of knowledge and realization that gradually accumulates in the heart like the gold deposited on the banks of the Jambū-nadī.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bless us to never lose the taste for this nectar, so rarely found in this material world and so readily available to us. Never let us become blinded and think that a higher bliss can be found somewhere else. Give us the strength to cleanse our hearts from all *anarthas* and become worthy to translate your books. Who but a surrendered devotee can work on your fathomless books? Although we need linguistic skills and technical knowledge for this service, without purity and love for your books we could not go on. Please arm us with the patience, determination, and enthusiasm to execute the *yajña* of book production.

The potency of your books is unlimited. Who can imagine the wonderful fruits that will grow from the millions of seeds planted all over the world? Your books will crack the materialistic foundation of today’s society and, like the heavenly *jambū* fruits, inundate the entire planet with rivers of nectar.

Your servants at the North European BBT offices in New Rādhā-kuṇḍa, Sweden.

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust (Spanish)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

For so long looking for my friends. For so long looking for one friend.

For so long looking for one “friend,” someone who responded exactly to my desires, who always gave me the words, glances, and gestures that I desired, who always understood my changes and never got angry with me, who always came whenever I needed him…

For so long looking for that “friend” who didn’t have anything else to do in his life than be my friend. For so long looking for that reflection, that shadow, that would always accompany me, without any conditions, wherever I was, wherever I went.

For so long crying lost in the forest, not knowing how to go back, how to love, so dark, so alone. Creating in my imagination friends, lovers, servants…So alone. Me. Alone. Proud. Desperate.

And now I’m told that no, it’s not like this. That I’m not so intelligent; that I’m not so important.

That I’m not so alone. That it’s not true.

I see everything very blurry. But you’re teaching me how to see. I feel you close to me. You’re not the “friend” I was expecting. I was looking for one, at least one, who danced to my music. But no. You’re bringing with you melodies that are making me dance. I was looking for a friend who just loved me. But you’re teaching me how to love. And as I’m loving you more, as I’m loving your Kṛṣṇa more, as I’m loving all these people who are with you more, I feel like awakening, I feel alive.

Whenever I take one of your books, what you tell me is so simple, so heavy, so true, that you fill my life with joy. I want to serve you, I want to be at your feet, I want...

I want to know you. Where are you? In your books? I’ll read them. In your service? I’ll engage myself with all my strength. Will you help me? I’m so lost. I need your help. I don’t want to lose you again. Please help me go back with you. My friend; my father; my master. I want to be with you.

Thank you, Prabhupāda, for your warm hand that guides and accompanies me. Please don’t allow, for any reason, that I let go of your hand.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, my savior.

Your servants at the Spanish BBT.

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from Other Sources

## Back to Godhead

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

From the beginning, you had great plans for preaching with your *Back to Godhead* magazine. At first you did everything alone, despite incredible hardships. Later you passed on to your disciples the responsibility for unfolding your plans, and over the years many sincere devotees have served you by producing and distributing *Back to Godhead*. Though unqualified both materially and spiritually, we, your present servants at *Back to Godhead,* feel fortunate to have been chosen to serve *Back to Godhead* and to be able follow in the footsteps of those who came before us. We pray for their mercy so that we can satisfy you in our humble efforts to bring out a magazine that pleases you.

Because we want to serve Your Divine Grace and not be a hindrance to your preaching efforts, we naturally wonder whether the current *Back to Godhead* meets with your approval. We know that in essence the magazine has not changed since you were with us, reviewing each issue and correcting us when we started to stray from your vision. But we also know that we have felt the need to change the magazine in small ways because your movement has grown—in some ways changed—and so has the reach of *Back to Godhead*. Today the magazine is read mostly by those already familiar with Kṛṣṇa consciousness, so we are trying to present the philosophy in a way interesting and valuable to them.

Because you warned us of our tendency to change things whimsically, we pray that you give us the intelligence to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the most effect way, without unnecessary change.

We like to think that when we hear from the many devotees who tell us they love *Back to Godhead* we are hearing from you, speaking through them. Please help us understand your desires—through your devotees or in any way you see fit—and please make sure we stay on the right track.

During the past year, we began printing *Back to Godhead* in India, thereby greatly increasing the number of magazines distributed there. We hope that this small step forward pleases you, and we pray that you will inspire us in new ways to expand the influence of your *Back to Godhead*.

Begging to remain  
Your eternal servants at Back to Godhead magazine.

## Bala Books

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

In 1975, a friend informed me that you would be present at the Ratha-yātrā festival in Philadelphia. Although he tried to explain the importance of your presence, I could not begin to understand or appreciate. Reluctantly I agreed to attend. As we waited for you to arrive, my friend insisted that I put *tilaka* on and told me that I should bow down when I saw you. I made up my mind that I was not a follower and that I would not bow down to anyone. Your car arrived, and as you stepped out, I stood as everyone else offered their obeisances. It appeared you were looking in my direction. I checked behind me to see who you were looking at, but no one was there. It felt like you looked at me forever, although it was just a few brief moments. Your glance was penetrating. It was stern but loving.
